- Adam implementation:
```
def adam_step(z, grad, m, v, t, lr, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
   m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
   v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
   b1=1-beta1**t; b2=1-beta2**t
   z = z - lr*(m/b1)/(sqrt(v/b2)+eps)
```
Need no detach. In loop, allocate m,v. Use in-place operations for speed.

- Learning rate schedule: Start lr=0.05, decay when no improve every N steps. Could also use clip on update max 0.5 to avoid explosion. We can clip z after step to [-40,40]. If z min very negative, p zeros. Good.

Let's formulate code:

```
def compute_q(p):
    n=p.size
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    # convolution coefficients can be slightly negative due to roundoff; clip small negatives? For nonnegative p should be nonneg. Use c=np.maximum(c,0)
    q=n*c
    return q
```

But if we clip negative, q sum maybe not n; OK.

`score_from_q(q) = 2.0 * float(q.max())` because q=n*c. Nice.

Gradient top:

```
def top_weights(q, K, tau):
   m=q.size
   if K >= m: idx=np.arange(m)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]
   qidx=q[idx]
   # sort descending maybe
   order=np.argsort(-qidx)
   idx=idx[order]
   qidx=q[idx]
   qmax=qidx[0]
   z=(qidx-qmax)/max(tau,1e-12)
   exp=np.exp(z)
   s=exp.sum()
   if s<=0: exp=np.zeros_like(qidx); exp[0]=1; s=1
   w=exp/s
   return idx,w
```

`compute_grad_h(idx,w,p)`:
```
n=p.size; pr=p[::-1]; h=np.zeros(n)
for r,ww in zip(idx,w):
   ww=float(ww)
   if ww < 1e-15: continue
   j0 = r - (n-1)
   if j0 <0: j0=0
   j1 = r +1
   if j1 > n: j1=n
   p0 = n-1-r+j0
   h[j0:j1] += ww * pr[p0:p0+(j1-j0)]
return h
```

Let's design optimizer functions:

```
def optimize_logsum(parent, n, deadline, rng, tau_start=0.3, tau_end=0.02, lr_start=0.05, lr_end=0.01, penalty_start=0.1, penalty_end=0.0, print_every=5000):
    p0=resize_sequence(parent,n)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p0,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    best_p=p0.copy(); best_score=score_from_q(compute_q(p0))
    m=...; m_arr?...
    m_state=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    t=0; step=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       frac=... adjust tau, lr, penalty
       zclip = np.clip(z, -40,40)
       p=softmax(zclip)
       q, qmax = compute_q_and_max(p)
       score=2*qmax
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: ...
       # weights
       if step % 2000 ==0 maybe print.
```

Adjusting hyperparameters over time: We can define phases by fraction of duration:
- 0-20%: tau=tau_start (0.25), penalty=1.0? broad flatten, lr=0.03.
- 20-70%: tau linearly to 0.03, penalty to 0.05, lr 0.02.
- 70-100%: tau=0.01-0.005, penalty=0, lr 0.005.

Let's detail dense logsum optimizer with penalty:

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(parent, n, duration_s, rng, tau_start=0.25, tau_mid=0.05, tau_end=0.008, lr_start=0.04, lr_end=0.004, pen_start=0.5, pen_mid=0.1, pen_end=0.0, print_every=2000):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p = resize_sequence(parent,n)
    if p is None: p=uniform
    z = logp_center(p)
    m_par=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); adam_t=0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_from_q(compute_q(p))
    step=0; no_improve=0; noise_amp=0.1
    while time.monotonic() < start+duration_s:
        rem = duration_s - elapsed
        frac=elapsed/(duration_s+1e-9)
        if frac <0.2: tau=tau_start; lr=lr_start; pen=pen_start
        elif frac <0.6: interpolate; 
        else: tau=tau_end; lr=max(lr_end, lr_start*(1-0.9*frac)); pen=0
        # occasionally reseed if stuck
        if no_improve > 4000:
            # choose strategy
            if rng.random()<0.5:
                z = logp_center(best_p) + rng.normal(0, noise_amp, n)

                z += rng.normal(0, noise_amp, n)
            z-=z.mean(); m_par*=0; v*=0; no_improve=0; noise_amp=min(0.8, noise_amp*1.3)
        # compute p
        zc=np.clip(z, -40,40)
        p=softmax(zc)
        q,qmax, L, F? We need compute q and maybe F for h. To avoid recomputing F for q and h? compute F once.
        L=next_pow2(2*n-1) # precompute outside
        F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
        c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:m_len]
        c=np.maximum(c,0?)
        q=n*c; qmax=q.max()
        score=2*qmax
        if score<best: update...
        # weights
        z_w=(q-qmax)/tau
        z_w=np.clip(z_w, -50, 0)
        e=np.exp(z_w); s=e.sum()
        if s==0: e[top]=1
        w=e/s
        if pen>0:
            target = n/(2*n-1)
            u = 0.5*w + pen*(q-target)  # maybe normalize?
            # scale u to max abs 1 perhaps
            maxu=np.max(np.abs(u)); if maxu>0: u/=maxu
        else: u=w
        upad=np.empty(L)? To avoid allocating every step? Allocate once `w_buffer=np.zeros(L)`. But need set zeros after m. We can use `w_buf[:m_len]=u[::-1]`? For j 0..n-1, yes h_j. So no reversal. Good. Use `w_buf[:m_len]=u`; set rest zeros. But we need reset rest each step: `w_buf[m_len:L]=0`. Could allocate new w_buf=np.zeros(L); `w_buf[:m_len]=u`.         H=np.fft.irfft(F * np.conj(W), L)

        # due to roundoff, h finite
        mean_h=float(np.dot(p,h))

        # optional add tiny entropy gradient if grad all zero:
        maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))

             grad /= maxg

Detailed `optimize_array`:

```
def optimize_array(parent, n, duration_s, rng, tag='', max_phases=True):
    n=max(16,int(n))
    if duration_s <=0: return parent, score
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=resize_sequence(parent,n)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1); m=2*n-1
    exp_buf=np.zeros(n); p_buf=np.empty(n)
    # precompute target
    target = n / m
    best_p=p.copy(); q0=compute_q(p); best_score=float(q0.max())*2
    m_adam=np.zeros(n); v_adam=np.zeros(n); t_adam=0
    step=0; no_improve=0; noise_amp=0.15
    print_every=2000 if n<=8192 else 4000
    while ...:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
       if elapsed>=duration_s: break
       frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(duration_s,1e-12))
       # hyperparameters via piecewise
       if frac < 0.10:
           tau=0.25; lr=0.045; pen=0.0; mode=2 # flatten
       elif frac <0.30:
           t=(frac-0.10)/0.20; tau=lerp(0.25,0.08,t); lr=lerp(0.045,0.03,t); pen=lerp(0.2,0.08,t); mode=1
       elif frac <0.60:
           t=(frac-0.30)/0.30; tau=lerp(0.08,0.02,t); lr=lerp(0.03,0.015,t); pen=lerp(0.08,0.0,t); mode=1
       elif frac <0.85:
           t=(frac-0.60)/0.25; tau=lerp(0.02,0.006,t); lr=lerp(0.015,0.008,t); pen=0.0; mode=0
       else:
           tau=max(0.003,0.006*(1.5-frac)*2?) ; lr=max(0.003,0.008*(1.2-frac)*?); pen=0; mode=0
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=None, budget_s=2350.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    raw_budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+max(0, raw_budget-30)
    # get initial sequences
    init_candidates=[]
    # height_sequence_1 global
    try: hs=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,...); if valid: init_candidates.append(hs)
    # uniform etc maybe
    # Choose main N based on budget
    if raw_budget >= 1800: N_fine=16384
    elif raw_budget >= 900: N_fine=8192
    else: N_fine=4096
    N_mid = N_fine; N_coarse = N_fine//4 if ...
    best_global = None; best_global_score=inf
    # First maybe optimize from a seed at N_coarse with explore
    if N_fine>=8192 and time<deadline-800:
       parent = choose_best_of(init_candidates resized N_coarse)
       p,s=optimize_array(parent, N_coarse, min(300, ...), rng, profile='explore')
       update best_global (score)
       # use p as parent for mid/fine
    # Mid N=8192 if fine 16384 and time
    if N_fine==16384 and time<deadline-700:
       parent = best_global (resized 8192) maybe choose best among init at 8192 and coarse
       p,s=optimize_array(parent, 8192, 600, rng, profile='polish' or 'explore'? maybe explore if score>1.6 else polish)
       update
    # Fine optimize
    parent = best_global resized N_fine
    p,s=optimize_array(parent, N_fine, remaining, rng, profile='polish' if good else 'explore')
    update
    # final: consider resizing best_global to N_fine? If bestGlobal length smaller, maybe score better; collect candidates:
    cands=[best_global, resize(best_global, N_fine), resize(best_global,8192), uniform 1024, hs, ...]
    # choose min by fast_score (and maybe exact if small)
    final_seq=...
    # call evaluate_sequence on final and maybe fallback if inf
    return final_seq
```

Detailed functions:

```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return None
    return a/s
```

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p=normalize_positive(seq)
    m=int(m)
    if p is None:
       return np.ones(m)/m
    n=p.size
    if n==m: return p.copy()
    if n==1: return np.full(m,1.0/m)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_positive(out)
```
Need `np.interp` returns nonnegative. Good. For large n, linspace each call overhead; few calls.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(arr):
    p=normalize_positive(arr)
    if p is None: return np.inf
    n=p.size
    if n<=0: return np.inf
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    cm=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cm)
```
Could use `np.maximum`. Good.

`compute_q`:
```
def compute_q(p):
    n=p.size
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    q=c*float(n)
    return q,L,F
```
Returning F to reuse in dense h. But in sparse, not need W. We can compute q and F in one go.

`score_from_q(q)`:
```
def score_from_q(q):
    qm=float(np.max(q)) if q.size else np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(qm): return np.inf
    return 2.0*qm
```

def make_probability(z, p_buf=None, work=None):

```
Maybe inline.

`sparse_h`:
```
def sparse_h(q, p, K, tau_w, h_buf):
    n=p.size; m=q.size
    h_buf.fill(0.0)
    if K >= m:
       weights = np.full(m,1.0/m) # but m large, not use
    if K <=0: return h_buf
    # argpartition
    if K >= m:
       idx=np.arange(m)
       weights=np.ones(m)/m
       # too slow if m large and K=m; not used
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]
       order=np.argsort(-q[idx])
       idx=idx[order]
       qidx=q[idx]
       z=(qidx - qidx[0]) / max(1e-12, tau_w)
       e=np.exp(z)
       s=e.sum()
       if s <= 0: e[0]=1.0; s=1.0
       weights=e/s
    pr=p[::-1]
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
       r=int(idx[ii]); ww=float(weights[ii])
       if ww < 1e-14: continue
       j0 = r - (n-1)
       if j0 <0: j0=0
       j1 = r + 1
       if j1 > n: j1=n
       p0 = n - 1 - r + j0
       length = j1 - j0
       if length >0:
           h_buf[j0:j1] += ww * pr[p0:p0+length]
    return h_buf
```
Using `idx` and weights arrays small. For K=m (not used), loops huge; avoid. We'll call K small.

Not needed if we compute grad immediately. For memory, H length L.

In optimizer, we need `F` from p. We compute `c` via `F*F`. For dense h, we also need W. For sparse h, no W. Good.

def logsum_weights(q, tau, w_buf):
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qmax):
       w_buf.fill(0); w_buf[0]=1; return qmax
    z = (q - qmax) / max(1e-12, tau)
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)  # but z is new array? q - qmax alloc. Could use buffer.
    e = np.exp(z, out=w_buf)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0:
        e.fill(0); e[int(np.argmax(q))]=1; s=1
    e /= s
    return qmax
```

Now, schedule with multiple lengths. Let's define `propose_candidate`:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=None, budget_s=2350.0, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    t_start=time.monotonic()

Now, `generate_seeds` function top-level:

```
def generate_seeds(n, rng, count=random?):
    res=[]
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t=(i+0.5)/float(n)
    # uniform
    res.append(np.full(n,1.0/n))
    # exponentials
    for a in (0.3,0.7,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0):
        for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
            p=np.exp(sign*a*t)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))
    # beta-like
    for a,b in [(1,0),(2,0),(0,1),(0,2),(1,1),(2,1),(1,2),(3,1),(1,3)]:
        p=(t+1e-3)**a * (1.0-t+1e-3)**b
        res.append(normalize_positive(p))
    # linear
    for alpha in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
        for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
            p=1.0 + sign*alpha*(2.0*t-1.0)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))
    # sine
    for alpha in (0.4,0.7,0.9):
        for phase in (0.0, np.pi/4, np.pi/2):
            p=1.0 + alpha*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + phase)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))
    # random smooth Fourier
    for _ in range(4):
        p=np.ones(n)
        for k in range(1,7):
            amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.6)
            ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
            p += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
        res.append(normalize_positive(p))
    return [x for x in res if x is not None]
```

Let's write `get_schedule`:

```
def lerp_val(a,b,t):
    if t < 0: t=0
    elif t>1: t=1
    return a + (b-a)*float(t)

def get_schedule(frac, profile):
    if profile == 'explore':
       if frac < 0.10:
          return 0.25, 0.05, 0.0, 2
       elif frac < 0.35:
          t=(frac-0.10)/0.25
          return lerp_val(0.25,0.05,t), lerp_val(0.05,0.025,t), lerp_val(0.8,0.15,t), 1
       elif frac < 0.65:
          t=(frac-0.35)/0.30
          return lerp_val(0.05,0.010,t), lerp_val(0.025,0.012,t), lerp_val(0.15,0.0,t), 1
       else:
          t=(frac-0.65)/0.35
          return lerp_val(0.010,0.003,t), lerp_val(0.012,0.004,t), 0.0, 0
    else:
       if frac < 0.15:
          t=frac/0.15
          return lerp_val(0.07,0.015,t), lerp_val(0.035,0.02,t), lerp_val(0.25,0.05,t), 1
       elif frac < 0.55:
          t=(frac-0.15)/0.40
          return lerp_val(0.015,0.005,t), lerp_val(0.02,0.009,t), lerp_val(0.05,0.0,t), 1
       else:
          t=(frac-0.55)/0.45
          return lerp_val(0.005,0.002,t), lerp_val(0.009,0.004,t), 0.0, 0
```
If profile unknown, polish.

In optimize, `mode` integers: 2 flatten (u=q), 1 mixed, 0 max. If mode 2, tau unused except maybe penalty? no.

Prealloc and loop. Need be careful with `q_buf` and `u_buf` when qmax 0. If p all zero? not. `qmax` small <=1. n large.

Let's draft optimize code in detail:

```
def optimize_array(parent, n, duration_s, rng, profile='polish', tag='opt'):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    duration_s = max(1.0, float(duration_s))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    p = resize_sequence(parent, n)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= z.mean()
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    m = 2*n - 1

    z_work = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p_buf = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    m_adam = np.zeros(n); v_adam = np.zeros(n); tmp = np.empty(n); m_hat = np.empty(n); v_hat = np.empty(n); update = np.empty(n)
    q_buf = np.empty(m); z_w = np.empty(m); w_m = np.empty(m); u_buf = np.empty(m); w_full = np.empty(L)
    h_buf = np.empty(n)
    grad_buf = np.empty(n)

    q0, _, _ = compute_q(p)
    best_score = score_from_q(q0)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_z = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300)); best_z -= best_z.mean()

    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    step=0; t_adam=0; no_improve=0; noise_amp=0.15
    print_every = 5000 if n <= 8192 else 8000

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        if elapsed >= duration_s: break
        frac = elapsed / duration_s
        tau, lr, pen, mode = get_schedule(frac, profile)
        # softmax
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        np.subtract(z, zmax, out=z_work)
        np.exp(z_work, out=p_buf)
        s = float(p_buf.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p_buf.fill(1.0/float(n)); s=1.0
        else:
            p_buf /= s

        F = np.fft.rfft(p_buf, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
        np.multiply(c[:m], float(n), out=q_buf)
        np.maximum(q_buf, 0.0, out=q_buf)
        # replace nonfinite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(q_buf)):
            q_buf = np.where(np.isfinite(q_buf), q_buf, 0.0)
        qmax = float(np.max(q_buf))
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            # reset
            np.copyto(z, best_z)
            z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, n)
            z -= z.mean()
            m_adam *= 0.1; v_adam *= 0.1
            continue
        score = 2.0 * qmax
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p_buf.copy()
            best_z = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
            best_z -= best_z.mean()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        # prepare u or sparse
        sparse = False
        if mode == 0 and tau < 0.015:
            sparse = True
        elif mode == 0:
            # dense
            pass

        if not sparse:
            if mode == 2:
                if qmax > 0:
                    np.divide(q_buf, qmax, out=u_buf)
                else:
                    u_buf.fill(0.0)
            else:
                tau_use = max(1e-4, tau)
                np.subtract(q_buf, qmax, out=z_w)
                z_w *= 1.0 / tau_use
                np.clip(z_w, -50.0, 0.0, out=z_w)
                np.exp(z_w, out=w_m)
                sw = float(w_m.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                    w_m.fill(0.0)
                    imax = int(np.argmax(q_buf))
                    w_m[imax] = 1.0
                    sw = 1.0
                else:
                    w_m /= sw
                if mode == 1:
                    if pen > 1e-8:
                        u_buf[:] = w_m
                        u_buf += pen * q_buf
                        umax = float(np.max(u_buf))
                        if umax > 0.0:
                            u_buf /= umax
                    else:
                        u_buf[:] = w_m
                else: # mode 0
                    u_buf[:] = w_m
            w_full.fill(0.0)
            w_full[:m] = u_buf
            W = np.fft.rfft(w_full, L)
            H = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conj(W), L)
            h = H[:n]
        else:
            h = sparse_h(q_buf, p_buf, 5, 0.02, h_buf)
            # h is h_buf

        mean_h = float(np.dot(p_buf, h))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_h):
            mean_h = 0.0
        np.subtract(h, mean_h, out=grad_buf)
        grad_buf *= p_buf

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_buf)))
        should_perturb = (frac > 0.2 and no_improve > 4000)
        if maxg < 1e-14 or should_perturb:
            if should_perturb:
                if score > best_score + 0.04 and rng.random() < 0.5:
                    np.copyto(z, best_z)
                else:
                    z += rng.normal(0.0, noise_amp, n)
                z -= z.mean()
                m_adam *= 0.1; v_adam *= 0.0
                no_improve = 0
                noise_amp = min(0.8, noise_amp * 1.2)
            # add random gradient
            rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n, out=grad_buf)
            grad_buf *= p_buf
            rg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_buf)))
            if rg > 0.0:
                grad_buf /= rg
            else:
                step += 1
                continue
        else:
            grad_buf /= maxg

        t_adam += 1
        np.multiply(grad_buf, grad_buf, out=tmp)
        tmp *= (1.0 - beta2)
        v_adam *= beta2
        v_adam += tmp
        grad_buf *= (1.0 - beta1)
        m_adam *= beta1
        m_adam += grad_buf
        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** t_adam
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** t_adam
        np.divide(m_adam, b1, out=m_hat)
        np.divide(v_adam, b2, out=v_hat)
        np.sqrt(v_hat, out=v_hat)
        v_hat += eps
        np.divide(m_hat, v_hat, out=update)
        z -= lr * update
        np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0, out=z)
        zr = float(z.mean())
        z -= zr

        step += 1
        if step % print_every == 0:
            print(f"{tag} n={n} step={step} score={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} tau={tau:.4f} pen={pen:.3f} mode={mode}")

    return best_p, best_score
```

Some concerns:
- `v_adam *= 0.0` in perturb sets zeros. Good.

Now, `choose_parents`:
```
def choose_parents(input_seqs, n, rng):
    cands=[]
    for arr in input_seqs:
       r=resize_sequence(arr,n)
       if r is not None: cands.append(r)
    seeds=generate_seeds(n,rng)
    cands.extend(seeds)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for c in cands:
       if c is None: continue
       sc=fast_score(c)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
          best=c; best_sc=sc
    if best is None:
       best=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=2.0
    return best, best_sc
```

Derivative of C_smooth = 2∑w q; ∂C/∂p = 4n h. Omit 4n. Log gradient = p*(4n h - p dot 4n h) = 4n p*(h-mean). Omit 4n. Adam normalized; direction same. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation uses FFT-based evaluation and optimization of
log-softmax variables.  It combines a flattening phase based on the
quadratic penalty sum_k q_k^2 (where q_k = n * (a*a)_k), a smoothed
max phase using log-sum-exp weights, and a final exact-max polishing
phase with a sparse top-coefficient gradient.  The sequence is further
refined in a coarse-to-fine length schedule and the best final length
is selected from several resized candidates.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def lerp_val(a, b, t):
    t = float(t)
    if t <= 0.0:
        return float(a)
    if t >= 1.0:
        return float(b)
    return float(a) + (float(b) - float(a)) * t


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p = normalize_positive(seq)
    m = int(m)
    if p is None:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return normalize_positive(out)


def compute_q(p):
    """Return q = n * (a * a) and the FFT of p."""
    n = p.size
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    q = c * float(n)
    return q, L, F


def score_from_q(q):
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qmax):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * qmax


def fast_score(arr):
    p = normalize_positive(arr)
    if p is None or p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    q, _, _ = compute_q(p)
    return score_from_q(q)


def sparse_h(q, p, K, tau_w, h_buf):
    """
    h_j = sum_r w_r p_{r-j}, where w is a softmax weight over the
    top-K coefficients of q.
    """
    n = p.size
    m = q.size
    h_buf.fill(0.0)
    if K <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return h_buf
    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
        weights = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -K)[-K:]
        idx = idx[np.argsort(-q[idx])]
        qidx = q[idx]
        z = (qidx - qidx[0]) / max(1e-12, float(tau_w))
        np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
        np.exp(z, out=z)
        s = float(z.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            z.fill(0.0)
            z[0] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
        weights = z / s

    pr = p[::-1]
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        r = int(idx[ii])
        ww = float(weights[ii])
        if ww < 1e-14:
            continue
        j0 = r - (n - 1)
        if j0 < 0:
            j0 = 0
        j1 = r + 1
        if j1 > n:
            j1 = n
        p0 = n - 1 - r + j0
        length = j1 - j0
        if length > 0:
            h_buf[j0:j1] += ww * pr[p0:p0 + length]
    return h_buf


def get_schedule(frac, profile):
    """
    Returns (tau, lr, penalty, mode).
    mode 2: flatten with u = q
    mode 1: mixed smoothed max + flattening/target penalty
    mode 0: smoothed max only
    """
    if profile == "explore":
        if frac < 0.10:
            return 0.25, 0.05, 0.0, 2
        if frac < 0.35:
            t = (frac - 0.10) / 0.25
            return (
                lerp_val(0.25, 0.05, t),
                lerp_val(0.05, 0.025, t),
                lerp_val(0.80, 0.15, t),
                1,
            )
        if frac < 0.65:
            t = (frac - 0.35) / 0.30
            return (
                lerp_val(0.05, 0.010, t),
                lerp_val(0.025, 0.012, t),
                lerp_val(0.15, 0.0, t),
                1,
            )
        t = (frac - 0.65) / 0.35
        return (
            lerp_val(0.010, 0.003, t),
            lerp_val(0.012, 0.004, t),
            0.0,
            0,
        )

    # polish profile
    if frac < 0.15:
        t = frac / 0.15
        return (
            lerp_val(0.07, 0.015, t),
            lerp_val(0.035, 0.02, t),
            lerp_val(0.25, 0.05, t),
            1,
        )
    if frac < 0.55:
        t = (frac - 0.15) / 0.40
        return (
            lerp_val(0.015, 0.005, t),
            lerp_val(0.02, 0.009, t),
            lerp_val(0.05, 0.0, t),
            1,
        )
    t = (frac - 0.55) / 0.45
    return (
        lerp_val(0.005, 0.002, t),
        lerp_val(0.009, 0.004, t),
        0.0,
        0,
    )


def optimize_array(parent, n, duration_s, rng, profile="polish", tag="opt", target_q=0.745):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    duration_s = max(1.0, float(duration_s))
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    p = resize_sequence(parent, n)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= z.mean()

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m = 2 * n - 1

    z_work = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p_buf = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m_adam = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_adam = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tmp = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m_hat = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_hat = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    update = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)

    q_buf = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    z_w = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w_m = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    u_buf = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w_full = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)

    h_buf = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    grad_buf = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)

    q0, _, _ = compute_q(p)
    best_score = score_from_q(q0)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_z = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
    best_z -= best_z.mean()

    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    step = 0
    t_adam = 0
    no_improve = 0
    noise_amp = 0.15
    print_every = 5000 if n <= 8192 else 8000

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        if elapsed >= duration_s:
            break

        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(duration_s, 1e-9))
        tau, lr, pen, mode = get_schedule(frac, profile)

        # softmax p from z, keeping z unchanged
        zmax = float(np.max(z))
        np.subtract(z, zmax, out=z_work)
        np.exp(z_work, out=p_buf)
        s = float(p_buf.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p_buf.fill(1.0 / float(n))
            s = 1.0
        else:
            p_buf /= s

        # q = n * p*p
        F = np.fft.rfft(p_buf, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
        np.multiply(c[:m], float(n), out=q_buf)
        q_buf = np.where(np.isfinite(q_buf), q_buf, 0.0)
        np.maximum(q_buf, 0.0, out=q_buf)

        qmax = float(np.max(q_buf))
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
            np.copyto(z, best_z)
            z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, n)
            z -= z.mean()
            m_adam *= 0.1
            v_adam *= 0.0
            step += 1
            continue

        score = 2.0 * qmax

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p_buf.copy()
            best_z = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300))
            best_z -= best_z.mean()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        # Decide whether to use a cheap sparse top-K gradient.
        use_sparse = (mode == 0 and tau < 0.015)

        if not use_sparse:
            if mode == 2:
                # Flatten: gradient of sum q_k^2.
                if qmax > 0.0:
                    np.divide(q_buf, qmax, out=u_buf)
                else:
                    u_buf.fill(0.0)
            else:
                # Log-sum-exp weights on q.
                tau_use = max(1e-4, float(tau))
                np.subtract(q_buf, qmax, out=z_w)
                z_w *= 1.0 / tau_use
                np.clip(z_w, -50.0, 0.0, out=z_w)
                np.exp(z_w, out=w_m)
                sw = float(w_m.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                    w_m.fill(0.0)
                    imax = int(np.argmax(q_buf))
                    w_m[imax] = 1.0
                    sw = 1.0
                else:
                    w_m /= sw

                if mode == 1:
                    if pen > 1e-8:
                        target_use = min(
                            float(target_q),
                            max(0.70, 0.5 * best_score - 0.005),
                        )
                        u_buf[:] = w_m
                        u_buf += (0.5 * pen) * q_buf
                        np.subtract(q_buf, target_use, out=z_w)
                        np.maximum(z_w, 0.0, out=z_w)
                        u_buf += (0.5 * pen) * z_w
                        umax = float(np.max(u_buf))
                        if umax > 0.0:
                            u_buf /= umax
                    else:
                        u_buf[:] = w_m
                else:
                    u_buf[:] = w_m

            w_full.fill(0.0)
            w_full[:m] = u_buf
            W = np.fft.rfft(w_full, L)
            H = np.fft.irfft(F * np.conj(W), L)
            h = H[:n]
        else:
            h = sparse_h(q_buf, p_buf, 5, 0.02, h_buf)

        mean_h = float(np.dot(p_buf, h))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_h):
            mean_h = 0.0

        np.subtract(h, mean_h, out=grad_buf)
        grad_buf *= p_buf

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_buf)))
        should_perturb = (frac > 0.2 and no_improve > 4000)

        if maxg < 1e-14 or should_perturb:
            if should_perturb:
                if score > best_score + 0.04 and rng.random() < 0.5:
                    np.copyto(z, best_z)
                else:
                    z += rng.normal(0.0, noise_amp, n)
                z -= z.mean()
                m_adam *= 0.1
                v_adam *= 0.0
                no_improve = 0
                noise_amp = min(0.8, noise_amp * 1.2)

            rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n, out=grad_buf)
            grad_buf *= p_buf
            rg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad_buf)))
            if rg > 0.0:
                grad_buf /= rg
            else:
                step += 1
                continue
        else:
            grad_buf /= maxg

        # Adam on log-probabilities.
        t_adam += 1
        np.multiply(grad_buf, grad_buf, out=tmp)
        tmp *= (1.0 - beta2)
        v_adam *= beta2
        v_adam += tmp

        grad_buf *= (1.0 - beta1)
        m_adam *= beta1
        m_adam += grad_buf

        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** t_adam
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** t_adam
        np.divide(m_adam, b1, out=m_hat)
        np.divide(v_adam, b2, out=v_hat)
        np.sqrt(v_hat, out=v_hat)
        v_hat += eps
        np.divide(m_hat, v_hat, out=update)

        z -= lr * update
        np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0, out=z)
        z -= float(z.mean())

        step += 1
        if step % print_every == 0:
            print(
                f"{tag} n={n} step={step} score={score:.6f} "
                f"best={best_score:.6f} tau={tau:.4f} pen={pen:.3f} mode={mode}"
            )

    return best_p, best_score


def generate_seeds(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    res = []

    res.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    for a in (0.3, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
            p = np.exp(sign * a * t)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))

    for a, b in ((1, 0), (2, 0), (0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 1), (2, 1), (1, 2), (3, 1), (1, 3)):
        p = (t + 1e-3) ** a * (1.0 - t + 1e-3) ** b
        res.append(normalize_positive(p))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
        for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
            p = 1.0 + sign * alpha * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))

    for alpha in (0.4, 0.7, 0.9):
        for phase in (0.0, np.pi / 4.0, np.pi / 2.0):
            p = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + phase)
            res.append(normalize_positive(p))

    for _ in range(4):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.6)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        res.append(normalize_positive(p))

    out = []
    for x in res:
        if x is not None and x.size == n:
            out.append(x)
    return out


def choose_parents(input_seqs, n, rng):
    cands = []
    for arr in input_seqs:
        r = resize_sequence(arr, n)
        if r is not None:
            cands.append(r)

    cands.extend(generate_seeds(n, rng))

    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for cand in cands:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = fast_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = 2.0
    return best, best_sc


def finalize_candidates(cands, prefer_small_margin=0.004):
    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_small = None
    best_small_sc = np.inf

    for cand in cands:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        p = normalize_positive(cand)
        if p is None:
            continue
        if p.size > 20000:
            continue
        sc = fast_score(p)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        if sc < best_sc:
            best = p
            best_sc = sc
            if p.size <= 8192:
                best_small = p
                best_small_sc = sc
        elif p.size <= 8192 and sc < best_small_sc:
            best_small = p
            best_small_sc = sc

    if best is None:
        return np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    if best.size > 8192 and best_small is not None and best_small_sc <= best_sc + prefer_small_margin:
        return best_small

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=None, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    # Accept alternative budget names just in case.
    for key in ("budget", "time_limit", "time", "seconds"):
        if key in kwargs and kwargs[key] is not None:
            budget_s = kwargs[key]
            break

    # Accept alternative seed names.
    if seed is None:
        for key in ("initial_sequence", "initial", "start", "parent", "seq"):
            if key in kwargs and kwargs[key] is not None:
                seed = kwargs[key]
                break

    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    raw_budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    final_deadline = t_start + max(1.0, raw_budget - 60.0)

    input_seqs = []

    if seed is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(seed, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-12:
                input_seqs.append(arr / float(arr.sum()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if hs is not None:
            hsa = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64)
            hsa = np.where(np.isfinite(hsa), hsa, 0.0)
            hsa = np.maximum(hsa, 0.0)
            if hsa.size > 0 and float(hsa.sum()) > 1e-12:
                input_seqs.append(hsa / float(hsa.sum()))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if raw_budget < 800.0:
        N_mid = 1024
        N_fine = 4096
    elif raw_budget < 1500.0:
        N_mid = 4096
        N_fine = 8192
    else:
        N_mid = 8192
        N_fine = 16384

    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf

    if N_fine == 16384:
        avail = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())
        dur_m = min(600.0, max(0.0, avail * 0.25))

        if dur_m > 120.0:
            par_m, sc_m = choose_parents(input_seqs, N_mid, rng)
            prof_m = "polish" if sc_m < 1.62 else "explore"
            par_m, sc_m = optimize_array(
                par_m, N_mid, dur_m, rng, profile=prof_m, tag="mid"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_m) and sc_m < best_s:
                best_p = par_m
                best_s = sc_m

        # Choose a fine parent.
        par_f_resized = None
        if best_p is not None:
            par_f_resized = resize_sequence(best_p, N_fine)
            if par_f_resized is None:
                par_f_resized = resize_sequence(input_seqs[0], N_fine)
        par_f_alt, sc_alt = choose_parents(input_seqs, N_fine, rng)

        if par_f_resized is None:
            par_f = par_f_alt
        else:
            sc_f1 = fast_score(par_f_resized)
            if not np.isfinite(sc_f1) or (np.isfinite(sc_alt) and sc_alt < sc_f1):
                par_f = par_f_alt
            else:
                par_f = par_f_resized

        prof_f = "polish" if (np.isfinite(best_s) and best_s < 1.62) else "explore"
        rem = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
        if rem > 120.0:
            par_f, sc_f = optimize_array(
                par_f, N_fine, rem, rng, profile=prof_f, tag="fine"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < best_s:
                best_p = par_f
                best_s = sc_f
    else:
        par_f, sc_f = choose_parents(input_seqs, N_fine, rng)
        prof_f = "polish" if sc_f < 1.62 else "explore"
        rem = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
        if rem > 60.0:
            par_f, sc_f = optimize_array(
                par_f, N_fine, rem, rng, profile=prof_f, tag="main"
            )
        if np.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < best_s:
            best_p = par_f
            best_s = sc_f

    cands = []
    if best_p is not None:
        cands.append(best_p)
        for m in (1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384):
            if m != best_p.size:
                r = resize_sequence(best_p, m)
                if r is not None:
                    cands.append(r)

    for arr in input_seqs:
        cands.append(arr)

    cands.append(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)

    final_p = finalize_candidates(cands, prefer_small_margin=0.004)
    final_p = np.asarray(final_p, dtype=np.float64)
    final_p = np.where(np.isfinite(final_p), final_p, 0.0)
    final_p = np.maximum(final_p, 0.0)

    s = float(final_p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-9:
        final_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        s = 1.0

    final_p = final_p / s

    # Light validation for small lengths; avoid expensive exact validation for large lengths.
    try:
        if final_p.size <= 4096:
            test_seq = [float(x) for x in final_p]
            test_sc = evaluate_sequence(test_seq)
            if not np.isfinite(test_sc):
                final_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
